SHEET: Read Me [state=]
A1=Data Dictionary - Thanushree (9 tables)
A2=Schema: core - prepared 07 Nov 2026 from the Thanushree data dictionary, Saamya_Authoritative_Data_Dictionary.xlsx and insert_complete_taxonomy_280.sql.
A4=What this is | B4=Every table, column and allowed value of the 9 tables: interest_categories, interest_subcategories, interests, profiles, user_interests, profile_prompts, notification_preferences, device_tokens and prompt_catalogue. The full interest catalog and all value lists are on their own sheets. Categories, subcategories and interests follow the order of the SQL seed script (Music, Dancing, Languages, Sports, Reading, Writing, Art & Drawing, Cooking, Acting, Movies, Shows & Entertainment).
A5=How to read it | B5=Tables -> Data Dictionary (every column) -> Value Lists (table > column > values; each value sits on its own row under the column, marked with an arrow) -> All Values (every list in one sheet) -> Interest Catalog -> Relationships.
A6=Common rules | B6=Status / type lists are named CHECKs. Every table has created_at / updated_at; updated_at is kept by the core.set_updated_at() trigger. ON DELETE RESTRICT on all links except device_tokens.user_id (CASCADE).
A7=Key words | B7=PK = primary key. FK = link to another table. UNIQUE = no duplicates. CHECK = rule the database enforces.
